event = {
	id = 6000
	country = REB
	persistent = yes

	trigger = {	building = { province = 6	type = ic value = 2 				  } #base 4
		NOT = { building = { province = 6	type = ic value = 0.7	when = now	} }
	}

	name = "AI_EVENT" #name = "Inverness damaged"

	date = { day = 1 month = january year = 1914 }
	offset = 10
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = march year = 1963 }

	action = { 
		command = { type = construct which = ic where = 6 value = -0.04 }
		command = { type = event which = 7000 where = REB when = 360  }
		command = { type = event which = 7000 where = REB when = 1800 }
		
		command = { type = setflag which = ENG_Ruin when = 1 value = 1 }
	}
}
